{"forms":[{"form":"things","tags":["plural"]},{"form":"thang","raw_tags":["used to denote a known fad or popular activity"],"tags":["alternative","pronunciation-spelling","slang","usually"]},{"form":"thin'","tags":["alternative","informal","pronunciation-spelling"]},{"form":"thinge","tags":["alternative","archaic"]},{"form":"thynge","tags":["alternative","obsolete"]},{"form":"ting","tags":["alternative"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["That which is considered to exist as a separate entity, object, quality or concept."]},{"glosses":["A word, symbol, sign, or other referent that can be used to refer to any entity."]},{"glosses":["An individual object or distinct entity."]},{"glosses":["Whatever can be owned."],"topics":["law"]},{"glosses":["Corporeal object."],"topics":["law"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Hold on, let me just grab my things.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Possessions or equipment; stuff; gear."],"tags":["in-plural"]},{"glosses":["The latest fad or fashion."],"raw_tags":["with the"],"tags":["dated"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Cheek kissing is a French thing; you get used to it after a while.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A custom or practice."],"tags":["informal"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Q: Bacon pie? Is that a thing? A: Yes, I checked into it, and it's a thing. Some people are even connoisseurs about it.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A genuine concept, entity or phenomenon; something that actually exists (often contrary to expectation or belief)."],"raw_tags":["with a"],"tags":["informal"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Could you get me a thing of apple juice at the store?","type":"example"},{"text":"I just ate a whole thing of jelly beans.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A unit or container, usually containing consumable goods."],"tags":["informal"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The car looks cheap, but the thing is, I have doubts about its safety.","type":"example"},{"text":"That's the thing: we don't know where he is.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A problem, dilemma, or complicating factor."],"tags":["informal"]},{"glosses":["A penis."],"tags":["euphemistic","slang"]},{"glosses":["A vulva or vagina."],"tags":["euphemistic","slang"]},{"examples":[{"text":"you poor thing","type":"example"},{"text":"sweet young thing","type":"example"},{"text":"she's a funny old thing, but her heart's in the right place","type":"example"},{"text":"I met a pretty blond thing at the bar","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A living being or creature."]},{"examples":[{"text":"Oh yeah, I'm supposed to promote that vision thing.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used after a noun to refer dismissively to the situation surrounding the noun's referent."]},{"examples":[{"text":"it's not really my thing","type":"example"}],"glosses":["That which is favoured; personal preference."],"raw_tags":["in the negative","used possessively"],"tags":["informal"]},{"examples":[{"text":"let me do my thing; I'm here doing my thing","type":"example"}],"glosses":["One's typical routine, habits, or manner."],"raw_tags":["used possessively","with \"do\""],"tags":["informal"]},{"glosses":["A public assembly or judicial council in a Germanic country."],"tags":["historical"]},{"glosses":["A romantic relationship."],"tags":["informal"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Are John and Jennifer a thing again?
